1) Use digital weighing scale.

2) Measure your body weight every morning after you go to the washroom.

3) Wear minimum clothes. ( Eg : male with sarong , female with night gown)

4) Should not eat or drink anything before measure your weight.

5) Send us scale image of your body weight every morning.

[2024-11-07, 6:58:23 AM] Ivanka Nutrinex: 🔹 *How to measure your resting heart rate?* 💓
           
▪️Place your index and middle finger on your wrist just below the thumb or along either side of your neck.

▪️Then you can feel your pulse. 

▪️Use a watch to count the number of beats for 30 seconds and double it to get your beats per minute. 

▪️Repeat a few times to ensure an accurate reading.

[2024-11-12, 7:19:10 PM] Ivanka Nutrinex: *Electrolytes drink*
➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖


Ginger juice 2 teaspoons ( 10ml)
Lime juice 4 teaspoons (20ml)
Table salt 1g 
Add above To 500ml of fltered water and mix well. 
Have to use within 24 hour after prepare . Otherwise refrigerate and can keep for 3 days. 
Use this drink for normal day to day practices instead of water. 


Composition of this home made electrolytes drink is almost similar to most of the expensive posh electrolytes drinks.

➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖

▪️ *First meal*

 *Using Coconut oil*

▫️ Kola mallum
~ *Adding scrapped coconut 3 tbsp*


▫️ Nelli/ Starfruits/ Strawberry 🍓 *4 - 5* 


▪️ *Dinner*
➖➖➖➖➖

▫️ _Flourless pizza 🍕_

*Ingredients:-*

▪️3 Eggs🥚(for pizza crust)
▪️1 tbsp Coconut oil
▪️Fried Chicken🍗 slices/  sliced sausages/ sliced bacon 🥓 *(150g)*
▪️Tomato slice 🍅
▪️Chopped  Onion rings🧅
▪️Chopped Capsicum
▪️ *30g* Shredded Mozzarella🧀
▪️salt and pepper to taste

♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️

*Method:-*

1) Make the egg pizza dough by putting 3 eggs into a bowl and season it with salt and pepper and beat well.

2) Put the pan on the stove and heat up 1 tbsp of coconut oil. Put the shredded chicken/  sausages pieces/ bacon  until lightly brown.  Take them out and keep aside

3) Then add onion rings, bellpepper, tomato, capsicum on the pan with heated oil and fry them well.

4) Pour the egg mixture 
close the pan with a lid and cook on low heat for few seconds. 

4) Then add shredded chicken/  sausages pieces/ bacon 🥓, tomato, onion, bellpepper, capsicum and shredded mozzarella cheese, and cook with the lid closed until the cheese melts.


🍕🍕🍕🍕🍕🍕🍕🍕🍕🍕🍕


▫️Mixed nuts  *30g*

[2024-11-14, 8:05:30 PM] Ivanka Nutrinex: ▪️ *Spinach Ham cheese Omelette*
➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖


*_Ingredients_*


▪️ Eggs  *Large  3*
▪️ Butter   *2  tbsp* 
▪️ Cheddar *or* Mozzarella  cheese  *50g*
▪️ Chicken  Ham  *100g finely chopped* 
▪️ Baby Spinach  *few Springs* 
▪️ Salt and  pepper  as per teste 
▪️ Water  *2  tbsp*




♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️


*_Method_*


1) Beat eggs and water in a bowl  until  blended.


2) Heat the butter in a nonstick  pan over  medium  high  heat until  hot. Tilt pan to coat bottom. Pour in  egg mixture. Mixture  should  set immediately at edges.


3) Gently  push cooked portions from edges toward the centrer with inverted Turner so that  uncooked  eggs can reach the hot pan surface continue cooking, tilting pan and gently moving  cooked portions as needed.


4) When top surface of eggs is thickened and no visible liquid Egg remains, season  with  salt  and pepper. Place the  cheese on one side of Omelette. Top with  Spinach  & chicken  ham fold Omelette in half  with  turner. With a quick flip of the wrist, turn a pan and invert or slide Omelette  onto  plate.  Serve immediately.


♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️

[2024-11-14, 8:06:52 PM] Ivanka Nutrinex: ▪️ *Chicken Sandwich*
➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖


*_Ingredients_*


▪️  Chicken   breast  boneless  *200g*

▪️  Cheddar  Cheese  Slices    *50g*
 
▪️   Lettuce  leave    *1*

▪️   Tomato    *1*

▪️   Mayonnaise     *1* 

▪️   Salt  &  Pepper  *as per  teste*

♾♾♾♾♾♾♾♾♾♾♾♾

*_Method_*

1)  Cut chicken breast lengthwise into half. Season it with salt and pepper on both the sides.


2)Heat olive oil in a frying pan over medium heat. Add chicken breast and cook for 5-6 minutes from both the sides.


3)Tansfer cooked chicken on a cutting board. Spread homemade mayo on each half side of the chicken.


4) Arrange lettuce, cheese slice and tomato slices on one half and season it with salt and pepper according to taste.


5)  Close the sandwich with another chicken half and your  no-bread chicken sandwich is ready!


6)  Serve hot with mint chutney

[2024-12-11, 1:34:25 PM] Ivanka Nutrinex: ▪️ *Tuna  Cutlets*
➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖


*_Ingredient*

▪️ Tuna  fish 🐟 *250g Boiled*
▪️ Olive oil   *1  tbsp*
▪️ Mayonnaise  *1/2  tbsp*
▪️ Salt  as per  teste requirements 
▪️ Garlic  Gloves  *3 to 4 minced*
▪️ large Eggs   *1 lightly  beaten* 
▪️ Green chilli  *3- 4 finely  chopped*
▪️ Black Pepper Powder  *1/4   tbsp*
▪️ Parsley   *1/2 Sprigs  finely chopped*
▪️ Musted cream  *1/2  tbsp*



♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️


*_Method_*


1) Preheat the  oven  to the "Keep  warm"  setting ( 150 - 170 degrees  F )


2) In a  medium  bowl, Use a fork to mix together the tuna, Mayonnaise, mustard  cream, minced  Garlic, Eggs, Green  chilli  chopped, Salt, Black  pepper  powder,  and parsley. 


3) Heat the Olive  oil  in  a large non stick skillet over medium  heat.


4) Measuring  1/4  Cup  of the mixture for each cake. Fry the  patties until  the  golden brown, about  three minutes  per each  side.


5) Keep the cooked tuna cakes in a warm oven while frying  other  cakes.


6) Serve it immediately.



♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️

[2024-12-18, 3:13:20 PM] Ivanka Nutrinex: ▪️ *Creamy  Chicken Tomato  Soup* 
➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖


*_Ingredients_*
➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖

▪️ Chicken  breast  boneless  *250g  cooked*
▪️ Tomato  *100g* 
▪️ Carrot  *1  chopped*
▪️ Chicken  broth  *1  Cup*
▪️ Onion  *1  chopped*
▪️ Garlic  *3-4  minced* 
▪️ Coconut  milk powder  *1  tbsp*
▪️ Oregano  *1/4  tbsp*
▪️ Cream  cheese  *25g* 
▪️ Salt  *as per required  quantity*
▪️ Olive  oil   *1  tbsp* 


♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️


*_Method_*
➖➖➖➖➖➖➖

1) In a  large pot Over medium  high heat, Combine  Olive  oil, Carrot,  onion  and  garlic. Cook, stirring occasionally until  Onion is soft 6 to  8 mins. Add 1 tbsp Coconut  milk  powder and stir  until Vegetable  are coated Creamy, then add  tomatoes. Add broth and  oregano and stir, breaking up Tomatoes. Bring to a boil, reduce heat to a simmer and cook 8 to 10 mins.


2) Working  in  a  small batches, puree soup in a blender of food  processor and transfer back to pot. Stir in cream  cheese  salt and Chicken and heat through. Season to taste  with salt  and ground pepper and serve garnished  with  Parsley.

[2024-12-20, 10:58:08 AM] Ivanka Nutrinex: ▪️ *Spinach Ham cheese Omelette*
➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖


*_Ingredients_*


▪️ Eggs  *Large  3*
▪️ Butter   *2  tbsp* 
▪️ Cheddar *or* Mozzarella  cheese  *50g*
▪️ Chicken  Ham  *100g finely chopped* 
▪️ Baby Spinach  *few Springs* 
▪️ Salt and  pepper  as per teste 
▪️ Water  *2  tbsp*




♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️


*_Method_*


1) Beat eggs and water in a bowl  until  blended.


2) Heat the butter in a nonstick  pan over  medium  high  heat until  hot. Tilt pan to coat bottom. Pour in  egg mixture. Mixture  should  set immediately at edges.


3) Gently  push cooked portions from edges toward the centrer with inverted Turner so that  uncooked  eggs can reach the hot pan surface continue cooking, tilting pan and gently moving  cooked portions as needed.


4) When top surface of eggs is thickened and no visible liquid Egg remains, season  with  salt  and pepper. Place the  cheese on one side of Omelette. Top with  Spinach  & chicken  ham fold Omelette in half  with  turner. With a quick flip of the wrist, turn a pan and invert or slide Omelette  onto  plate.  Serve immediately.


♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️

[2024-12-20, 11:00:32 AM] Ivanka Nutrinex: ▪️ *Paneer  Soup*
➖➖➖➖➖➖➖


*_Ingredients_*



▪️ Paneer Cubes  *150g* 
▪️ Cabbage   *1  small head* 
▪️ Butter   *1  tbsp*
▪️ Garlic  minced  *2  tbsp* 
▪️ Ginger  minced  *1   tbsp*
▪️ Coconut  Oil  *1  tbsp*
▪️ Mustard Seeds *1/2  tbsp*
▪️ Salt & Pepper  as  per  teste 
▪️ Curry  leaves  *few Springs* 
▪️ Water  *4  Cups*



♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️


*_Method_*



1)  Take a Saucepan, heat the oil and add mustard seeds.


2)  When  the seeds Crackle, add the Curry leaves and crushed  Ginger and Garlic and saute for few minutes.


3) Now, add the Paneer and toss well.


4) Add 4 cups  of  water, Pepper and salt, mix well and add the chopped Cabbage.


5) Cover  with  the lid and simmer for 10 minutes. Add  Butter  and  serve  it  hot.

[2024-12-20, 1:15:41 PM] Ivanka Nutrinex: ▪️ *Paneer  Soup*
➖➖➖➖➖➖➖


*_Ingredients_*



▪️ Paneer Cubes  *150g* 
▪️ Cabbage   *1  small head* 
▪️ Butter   *1  tbsp*
▪️ Garlic  minced  *2  tbsp* 
▪️ Ginger  minced  *1   tbsp*
▪️ Coconut  Oil  *1  tbsp*
▪️ Mustard Seeds *1/2  tbsp*
▪️ Salt & Pepper  as  per  teste 
▪️ Curry  leaves  *few Springs* 
▪️ Water  *4  Cups*



♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️


*_Method_*



1)  Take a Saucepan, heat the oil and add mustard seeds.


2)  When  the seeds Crackle, add the Curry leaves and crushed  Ginger and Garlic and saute for few minutes.


3) Now, add the Paneer and toss well.


4) Add 4 cups  of  water, Pepper and salt, mix well and add the chopped Cabbage.


5) Cover  with  the lid and simmer for 10 minutes. Add  Butter  and  serve  it  hot.

➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖
[2025-01-09, 1:04:25 PM] Ivanka Nutrinex: ▪️ *Tuna  Cutlets*
➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖


*_Ingredient*

▪️ Tuna  fish 🐟 *300g Boiled*
▪️ Olive oil   *1  tbsp*
▪️ Mayonnaise  *1/2  tbsp*
▪️ Salt  as per  teste requirements 
▪️ Garlic  Gloves  *3 to 4 minced*
▪️ large Eggs   *1 lightly  beaten* 
▪️ Green chilli  *3- 4 finely  chopped*
▪️ Black Pepper Powder  *1/4   tbsp*
▪️ Parsley   *1/2 Sprigs  finely chopped*
▪️ Musted cream  *1/2  tbsp*



♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️


*_Method_*


1) Preheat the  oven  to the "Keep  warm"  setting ( 150 - 170 degrees  F )


2) In a  medium  bowl, Use a fork to mix together the tuna, Mayonnaise, mustard  cream, minced  Garlic, Eggs, Green  chilli  chopped, Salt, Black  pepper  powder,  and parsley. 


3) Heat the Olive  oil  in  a large non stick skillet over medium  heat.


4) Measuring  1/4  Cup  of the mixture for each cake. Fry the  patties until  the  golden brown, about  three minutes  per each  side.


5) Keep the cooked tuna cakes in a warm oven while frying  other  cakes.


6) Serve it immediately.

[2025-02-17, 11:51:37 AM] Ivanka Nutrinex: 🔵   *Hot Butter Mushroom* 


*_Ingredient _*


▪️  Mushroom  *150g*
▪️  Chili powder Pepper   *1   tbsp*
▪️  Chilli pieces  *1/2  tbsp*
▪️  Curry  Powder  *1/2  teaspoon*
▪️  Salt powder  *As per  teste*
▪️  Ginger & Garlic  paste  *1  tbsp*
▪️  Coconut oil  *1  tbsp*
▪️  Coconut  Milk  *1  Cup*
▪️  Butter  *1  tbsp*
▪️  Tomato  *1* 
▪️ Pepper  Powder   *1  tbsp*


♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️


*_Method_* 



▪️  Tear the Mushrooms  into small pieces. Then add chili powder, pepper powder, salt  and mix well. 


▪️Place a pot on the stove and fry the Mushrooms  in deep oil until golden. 


▪️Then place a separate vessel on the stove and add a little more butter and heat it. 


▪️After the butter melts, add crushed ginger & garlic paste  and curry powder. After it binds, add pieces of chillies, add half a cup less milk and tomato sauce to it. 


▪️After that, add salt to the required seasoning and after it boils well, add the fried Mushrooms, stir and cook.


▪️ Hot Butter  Mushroom  is Ready  to teste.


♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️

[2025-03-17, 1:17:26 PM] Ivanka Nutrinex: *Cheesy onion-garlic soup*🍲
_________________________________

 _Ingredients_
(per 1 serving)
~One big onion🧅
~4 garlic cloves
~ Cheese 🧀40g(optional)
~1 tbsp butter🧈
~chopped springs onions/scallions
~chopped carrot🥕 cubes
~salt and pepper
_________________________________

~Chop onions and garlic. Heat up 1 tbsp of butter in a pan & add garlic and onion. Stir well & it becomes tender. Then add cheese and stir well until it melts.

~Add a cup of water. Then put the mixture into a blender and blend well.

~Add the paste to a cooking pot. Add water (1 cup) or as required and carrot cubes. Salt and pepper to taste. Cook for 10 mins

~Serve it to a cup and sprinkle some chopped scallions/spring onions on top. Enjoy😋
__________________________________

[2025-03-17, 1:18:25 PM] Ivanka Nutrinex: ▪️ *Tuna  Cutlets*
➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖➖


*_Ingredient*

▪️ Tuna  fish 🐟 *250g Boiled*
▪️ Olive oil   *1  tbsp*
▪️ Mayonnaise  *1/2  tbsp*
▪️ Salt  as per  teste requirements 
▪️ Garlic  Gloves  *3 to 4 minced*
▪️ large Eggs   *1 lightly  beaten* 
▪️ Green chilli  *3- 4 finely  chopped*
▪️ Black Pepper Powder  *1/4   tbsp*
▪️ Parsley   *1/2 Sprigs  finely chopped*
▪️ Musted cream  *1/2  tbsp*



♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️


*_Method_*


1) Preheat the  oven  to the "Keep  warm"  setting ( 150 - 170 degrees  F )


2) In a  medium  bowl, Use a fork to mix together the tuna, Mayonnaise, mustard  cream, minced  Garlic, Eggs, Green  chilli  chopped, Salt, Black  pepper  powder,  and parsley. 


3) Heat the Olive  oil  in  a large non stick skillet over medium  heat.


4) Measuring  1/4  Cup  of the mixture for each cake. Fry the  patties until  the  golden brown, about  three minutes  per each  side.


5) Keep the cooked tuna cakes in a warm oven while frying  other  cakes.


6) Serve it immediately.



♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️♾️

[2025-03-22, 11:20:28 AM] Ivanka Nutrinex: *Cheesy onion-garlic soup*🍲
_________________________________

 _Ingredients_
(per 1 serving)
~One big onion🧅
~4 garlic cloves
~ Cheese 🧀40g(optional)
~1 tbsp butter🧈
~chopped springs onions/scallions
~chopped carrot🥕 cubes
~salt and pepper
_________________________________

~Chop onions and garlic. Heat up 1 tbsp of butter in a pan & add garlic and onion. Stir well & it becomes tender. Then add cheese and stir well until it melts.

~Add a cup of water. Then put the mixture into a blender and blend well.

~Add the paste to a cooking pot. Add water (1 cup) or as required and carrot cubes. Salt and pepper to taste. Cook for 10 mins

~Serve it to a cup and sprinkle some chopped scallions/spring onions on top. Enjoy😋
__________________________________
